                                         RecItem_G.GET(RecTransferRcptLine_G."Item No.");
                                         ItemCode_G := RecTransferRcptLine_G."Item No.";
                                         ItemDescription_G := RecItem_G.Description;
                                         IF RecTransferRcptLine_G."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           ItemCode_G += ' ' + RecTransferRcptLine_G."Variant Code";
                                           RecItemVariant_G.GET(RecTransferRcptLine_G."Item No.",RecTransferRcptLine_G."Variant Code");
                                           ItemDescription_G := RecItemVariant_G.Description;
                                          END;
                                         cduItemTrackingMgt_G.RetrieveILEFromShptRcpt(BufferILE_G,DATABASE::"Transfer Receipt Line",
                                           0,"Source Document No.",'',0,RecTransferRcptLine_G."Line No.");
                                         BufferILE_G.RESET;
                                         IF BufferILE_G.FINDSET THEN
                                           REPEAT
                                             IF BufferILE_G."Expiration Date" <> 0D THEN
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '(' +
                                                 FORMAT(BufferILE_G."Expiration Date") +')' + '|'
                                             ELSE
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '|';
                                           UNTIL BufferILE_G.NEXT = 0;
                                         IF LotValue_G <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           LotCaption_G := Text8053008;
                                           LotValue_G  := COPYSTR(LotValue_G,1,STRLEN(LotValue_G) - 1);
                                         END;
                                       END;
                                   END;
                                 END;
                                 // --> 20131002-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL  ne pas supprimer pour appro
                                IF "Manual Weighing No." <> '' THEN
                                   IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('WMS-RCPT-TICKMAOBLIG') THEN BEGIN
                                     ManualWeighingNoCaption_G := FIELDCAPTION("Manual Weighing No.")+ ' : ';
                                     ManualWeighingNoValue_G := "Manual Weighing No.";
                                   END;
                                 // <-- 20131002-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL  ne pas supprimer pour appro
                               END;
                             "Source Document Type"::"Warehouse Shipment" :  // Exp'dition Entrepot

                               BEGIN
                             Title_G:=FORMAT("Source Document Type"::"Warehouse Shipment");
                                 RecPostedWhseShptLine_G.RESET;
                                 RecPostedWhseShptLine_G.SETCURRENTKEY("No.","Weighing No.");
                                 RecPostedWhseShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Weighing No.","Entry No.");
                                 IF RecPostedWhseShptLine_G.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                                   DocOrigin_G := FORMAT(RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Source Document");
                           // Access pour Code Transporteur //ESR
                                   TransporteurCode_G :=RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Actual Shipping Agent Code";
                                   IF TableShippingAgent_G.GET(RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Actual Shipping Agent Code")   THEN BEGIN
                                     TransporteurName_G  := TableShippingAgent_G.Name;
                                   END;
                            // ESR
                                   IF NOT RecLocation_G.GET("Location Code") THEN
                                     CLEAR(RecLocation_G);
                                   Dest_G := "Location Code";
                                   Exp_G := "Source No.";
                                   AddrDestTel_G := STRSUBSTNO('%1%2', TextPhone, RecLocation_G."Phone No.");
                                   AddrDestFax_G := STRSUBSTNO('%1%2',TextFax, RecLocation_G."Fax No.");

                                   cduFormatAddr_G.Location(AddrDest_G, RecLocation_G);


                                   cduFormatAddr_G.FormatAddr(AddrArray_G,Name,"Name 2",'',Address,"Address 2"
                                    ,City,"Post Code",County,"Country/Region Code");

                                   CASE RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Source Document" OF
                                     RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Source Document"::"Sales Order" :
                                       BEGIN
                                         RecSalesShptLine_G.RESET;
                                         RecSalesShptLine_G.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.","Weighing No.");
                                         RecSalesShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Document No.","Source Document No.");
                                         RecSalesShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Weighing No.","Entry No.");
                                         RecSalesShptLine_G.FINDFIRST;
                                         RecItem_G.GET(RecSalesShptLine_G."No.");
                                         ItemCode_G := RecSalesShptLine_G."No.";
                                         ItemDescription_G := RecItem_G.Description;
                                         IF RecSalesShptLine_G."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           ItemCode_G += ' '+ RecSalesShptLine_G."Variant Code";
                                           RecItemVariant_G.GET(RecSalesShptLine_G."No.",RecSalesShptLine_G."Variant Code");
                                           ItemDescription_G := RecItemVariant_G.Description;
                                         END;
                                         cduItemTrackingMgt_G.RetrieveILEFromShptRcpt(BufferILE_G,DATABASE::"Sales Shipment Line",
                                           0,"Source Document No.",'',0,RecSalesShptLine_G."Line No.");
                                         BufferILE_G.RESET;
                                         IF BufferILE_G.FINDSET THEN
                                           REPEAT
                                             IF BufferILE_G."Expiration Date" <> 0D THEN
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '(' +
                                                 FORMAT(BufferILE_G."Expiration Date") +')' + '|'
                                             ELSE
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '|';
                                           UNTIL BufferILE_G.NEXT = 0;
                                         IF LotValue_G <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           LotCaption_G := Text8053008;
                                           LotValue_G  := COPYSTR(LotValue_G,1,STRLEN(LotValue_G) - 1);
                                         END;
                                       END;
                                     RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Source Document"::"Purchase Return Order" :
                                       BEGIN
                                         RecReturnShptLine_G.RESET;
                                         RecReturnShptLine_G.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.","Weighing No.");
                                         RecReturnShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Document No.","Source Document No.");
                                         RecReturnShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Weighing No.","Entry No.");
                                         RecReturnShptLine_G.FINDFIRST;
                                         RecItem_G.GET(RecReturnShptLine_G."No.");
                                         ItemCode_G := RecReturnShptLine_G."No.";
                                         ItemDescription_G := RecItem_G.Description;
                                         IF RecReturnShptLine_G."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           ItemCode_G += ' '+ RecReturnShptLine_G."Variant Code";
                                           RecItemVariant_G.GET(RecReturnShptLine_G."No.",RecReturnShptLine_G."Variant Code");
                                           ItemDescription_G := RecItemVariant_G.Description;
                                         END;
                                         cduItemTrackingMgt_G.RetrieveILEFromShptRcpt(BufferILE_G,DATABASE::"Return Shipment Line",
                                           0,"Source Document No.",'',0,RecReturnShptLine_G."Line No.");
                                         BufferILE_G.RESET;
                                         IF BufferILE_G.FINDSET THEN
                                           REPEAT
                                             IF BufferILE_G."Expiration Date" <> 0D THEN
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '(' +
                                                 FORMAT(BufferILE_G."Expiration Date") +')' + '|'
                                             ELSE
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '|';
                                           UNTIL BufferILE_G.NEXT = 0;
                                         IF LotValue_G <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           LotCaption_G := Text8053008;
                                           LotValue_G  := COPYSTR(LotValue_G,1,STRLEN(LotValue_G) - 1);
                                         END;
                                       END;
                                     RecPostedWhseShptLine_G."Source Document"::"Outbound Transfer" :
                                       BEGIN
                                         RecTransferShptLine_G.RESET;
                                         RecTransferShptLine_G.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.","Weighing No.");
                                         RecTransferShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Document No.","Source Document No.");
                                         RecTransferShptLine_G.SETRANGE("Weighing No.","Entry No.");
                                         RecTransferShptLine_G.FINDFIRST;
                                         RecItem_G.GET(RecTransferShptLine_G."Item No.");
                                         ItemCode_G := RecTransferShptLine_G."Item No.";
                                         ItemDescription_G := RecItem_G.Description;
                                         IF RecTransferShptLine_G."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           ItemCode_G += ' ' + RecTransferShptLine_G."Variant Code";
                                           RecItemVariant_G.GET(RecTransferShptLine_G."Item No.",RecTransferShptLine_G."Variant Code");
                                           ItemDescription_G := RecItemVariant_G.Description;
                                          END;
                                         cduItemTrackingMgt_G.RetrieveILEFromShptRcpt(BufferILE_G,DATABASE::"Transfer Shipment Line",
                                           0,"Source Document No.",'',0,RecTransferShptLine_G."Line No.");
                                         BufferILE_G.RESET;
                                         IF BufferILE_G.FINDSET THEN
                                           REPEAT
                                             IF BufferILE_G."Expiration Date" <> 0D THEN
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '(' +
                                                 FORMAT(BufferILE_G."Expiration Date") +')' + '|'
                                             ELSE
                                               LotValue_G += BufferILE_G."Lot No." + '|';
                                           UNTIL BufferILE_G.NEXT = 0;
                                         IF LotValue_G <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                           LotCaption_G := Text8053008;
                                           LotValue_G  := COPYSTR(LotValue_G,1,STRLEN(LotValue_G) - 1);
                                         END;
                                       END;
                                   END;
                                 END;
                                 // --> 20131002-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL  ne pas supprimer pour appro
                                IF "Manual Weighing No." <> '' THEN
                                   IF cduAdvancedSetupManagement_L.GetAdvancedParameterValueBool('WMS-EXPE-TICKMAOBLIG') THEN BEGIN
                                     ManualWeighingNoCaption_G := FIELDCAPTION("Manual Weighing No.")+ ' : ';
                                     ManualWeighingNoValue_G := "Manual Weighing No.";
                                   END;
                                 // <-- 20131002-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL ne pas supprimer pour appro
                               END;
                           END;
                           {
                           cduFormatAddr_G.FormatAddr(AddrArray_G,Name,"Name 2",'',Address,"Address 2"
                             ,City,"Post Code",County,"Country/Region Code");
                           }
                           // --> 20140212-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR  NE Pas faire pour Appro
                           IF( "Source Document Type" = "Source Document Type"::"Warehouse Shipment") OR      // -ESR
                             ( "Source Document Type" = "Source Document Type"::"Warehouse Receipt") OR ( SortieCeremis_G <> '')
                           THEN BEGIN   // -ESR
                           END ELSE BEGIN    // -ESR
                             IF ItemCode_G <> '' THEN  BEGIN
                               RecItem_G.GET(ItemCode_G);
                               IF CMCSpecies_G.GET(RecItem_G."Species Code") THEN   BEGIN
                                 ItemCode_G := CMCSpecies_G.Code;
                                 ItemDescription_G := CMCSpecies_G.Name;
                               END;
                             END;
                           END; // -ESR
                         END;

        ReqFilterFields=Source Document Type,Source Document No.;

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=1;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemVarName=NbCopie;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                            // BEfore
                            // SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 3);
                            // AFTER
                        IF BoolExpUniq  THEN BEGIN
                          SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 1);
                        END ELSE BEGIN
                          IF RecLocation_G."Computer Link Site/Siege" THEN BEGIN
                            CASE TRUE OF
                              "CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type" IN
                        // before           ["CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Receipt",
                               ["CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Delivery" ] :
                               BEGIN
                                SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 4);
                               END;
                            ELSE BEGIN
                              CASE TRUE OF
                                   "CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type" IN
                                   ["CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Transfer Delivery",
                                    "CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Resumption Receipt",
                                    "CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Resumption Delivery"] :
                                    BEGIN
                                     SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 3);
                                    END;
                                ELSE BEGIN
                                  CASE TRUE OF
                                     "CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type" IN
                                     ["CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Transfer Receipt"] :
                                      BEGIN
                                       SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 2);
                                      END;
                                     ELSE BEGIN
                        //after
                                       CASE TRUE OF
                                        "CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type" IN
                                        ["CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge"."Source Document Type"::"Collect Receipt"] :
                                         BEGIN
                        //                 IF (RecPostCollectReceipt_G."Species Code" = 'BLET')  AND ( RecLocation_G."Machine Protein") THEN BEGIN
                                          IF ( RecLocation_G."Machine Protein") THEN BEGIN
                                           SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 3);
                                         END ELSE BEGIN
                                           SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 4);
                                         END;
                                       END;
                                         ELSE BEGIN
                        // fin after
                                          SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 1);
                                          END;
                                        END;
                                      END;
                                    END;
                                  END;
                                END;
                              END;
                            END;
                          END ELSE BEGIN
                          SETRANGE(Num'ro, 1, 2);
                          END;
                        END;
                      END;

        DataItemLinkReference=CMC Post. Weighing Weighbridge;
      }

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function
